# Verified 3-domain motion-planner system on ∂Δ⁵ (S⁴): SC_strict ≤ 2

Source: `results/s4_starcover` (summary.json, cover.txt, planners.txt), re-verified here with the plain Definition 2.3 check (`gpu_sc.verify_system`) and audited by `Abstract_Motion_GPU/independent_check.py`.

## How it was obtained

Explicit construction, no search (optimized_algorithms.md §16, §19, §20): for a vertex `v` of `K = ∂Δ⁵`, `star(v)` consists of the 5 facets containing `v`, and `D(v) = star(v) × star(v)` (25 facet pairs × 70 staircase paths = 1750 facets) is a contiguity domain with the two-link chain `π₁ ∼ c_v ∼ π₂` (`c_v` = constant map to `v`): on a facet `σ` over the pair `(s, t)`, `π₁(σ) ∪ c_v(σ) = s` and `c_v(σ) ∪ π₂(σ) = t` are simplices (Theorem 1). The three rectangles `D(0), D(1), D(2)` cover every pair `(s, t)` of facets, because a pair is missed only if `{0, 1, 2}` is contained in the two vertices omitted by `s` and `t`, which is impossible (Corollary 3). Facets already covered by an earlier rectangle are removed, giving disjoint parts of 1750, 630 and 140 facets. Built by `python star_cover.py s4 results/s4_starcover 0,0 1,1 2,2` (verified with the plain Definition 2.3 check) and audited by `independent_check.py` (exit 0), all in under a second of CPU time. Since `TC(S⁴) = 2` (Farber) and `TC ≤ SC_strict`, this gives `SC_strict(∂Δ⁵) = 2`, sharp at subdivision level 0. Re-create with `bash reproduce_all.sh`.

## Setup

`K` = ∂Δ⁵ (S⁴): 6 vertices `0..5`, 6 facets:

```
  facet  0: [0, 1, 2, 3, 4]
  facet  1: [0, 1, 2, 3, 5]
  facet  2: [0, 1, 2, 4, 5]
  facet  3: [0, 1, 3, 4, 5]
  facet  4: [0, 2, 3, 4, 5]
  facet  5: [1, 2, 3, 4, 5]
```

`K × K` is the staircase (ordered) product on the `6 × 6` grid; grid vertex `(a, b)` has id `6a + b`; the 2520 maximal facets have 9 vertices. Facet ids enumerate pairs of facets `(i, j)` of `K` (`i` slowest), then the staircase paths as bit words with as many 1‑bits as `dim(facet_j)` in increasing numerical order, read from the lowest bit (0 = step in the first factor, 1 = step in the second). `π₁(a, b) = a`, `π₂(a, b) = b`.

## Summary

| part | facets | chain links |
|---:|---:|---:|
| J0 | 1750 | 2 |
| J1 | 630 | 2 |
| J2 | 140 | 2 |

Total 2520 = 2520 facets, pairwise disjoint.


## Part J0 — 1750 facets, 2 links

Planner maps on the vertices of J0 ('@' = grid vertex not in the domain); rows `a = 0..5`, columns `b = 0..5`. Map 0 is `π₁`, the last map is `π₂`; consecutive maps are 1‑contiguous on every facet of J0.
